## 👤 User *(22:15:10)*

# 🚀 MISSION: PRODUCTION ACTIVATION & REVENUE WIRING (PHASE BETA FINAL)

Lead Architect, a infraestrutura Neural e de Monetização foi codificada. Agora, tua missão é realizar o deploy físico, ativar a busca vetorial e conectar a cobrança de mcoCoins ao fluxo de orquestração real.

### 1. PRODUCTION DEPLOYMENT (ORACLE/SUPABASE)
- **Database:** Executa `supabase db push` para aplicar o índice HNSW e a função `deduct_mco_coins`.
- **Secrets:** Solicita ao Diretor a `OPENROUTER_API_KEY` e a `STRIPE_SECRET_KEY` (se ainda não estiverem no vault) e executa `supabase secrets set`.
- **Functions:** Realiza o deploy da Edge Function: `supabase functions deploy embed-mcorch-node`.

### 2. NEURAL ACTIVATION (KNOWLEDGE HARVESTING)
- **Backfill:** Cria um script temporário para percorrer os 6 nós existentes em `mcorch_nodes` e disparar a Edge Function `embed-mcorch-node` para cada um, gerando os embeddings iniciais.
- **Trigger Logic:** Garanta que, a partir de agora, toda criação de nó via `useMcorchMemory` ou `/memory summarize` dispare automaticamente o processo de embedding.

### 3. REVENUE WIRING (mcoCoins GATEKEEPER)
- **Integration:** Localiza o motor de orquestração (provavelmente `src/lib/orchestration.ts` ou `supabase/functions/orchestrate-content/`).
- **Action:** Injeta a função `checkAndDeductCoins` de `src/lib/billing.ts` no início do processo.
- **Rule:** O sistema deve abortar e informar "Saldo insuficiente" se o `profiles.mco_balance` for menor que `COIN_COSTS.ORCHESTRATION_RUN`.

### 4. MEMORY DECAY (FSRS-6 STABILITY)
- **Database:** Adiciona uma coluna `stability_score` (float, default 1.0) na tabela `mcorch_nodes`.
- **Logic:** Cria uma SQL function ou cron job que reduza levemente o `stability_score` de nós não acessados (baseado em `last_seen_at`), permitindo que a visualização do Grafo priorize "estrelas" mais brilhantes/recentes.

### 5. UI POLISH & DASHBOARD LIVE
- **Validation:** Verifica se o `MemoryGraphWidget.tsx` com `d3-force` está renderizando sem crashes após o build.
- **HUD:** Exibe o saldo de `mco_balance` do usuário em destaque no cabeçalho do dashboard.

### 6. OUTPUT: SYNC UPDATE
- Finaliza com `/memory summarize`.
- Realiza o commit final: `feat(beta): revenue integration and neural activation`.
- Atualiza o `HANDOFF.md` com: "Phase Beta Sealed. System Ready for Commercial Onboarding."

**DIRECTIVA:** Execução autónoma. Sem introduções. Modo "High effort" ativo. Foco em tornar o SaaS funcional para o primeiro cliente real.

>
> ```
> 1	import type { SupabaseClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js';
> 2	
> 3	// mcoCoins cost per operation — single source of truth
> 4	export const COIN_COSTS = {
> 5	  ORCHESTRATION_RUN: 10,
> 6	  CONTENT_GENERATION: 5,
> 7	  IMAGE_GENERATION: 3,
> 8	  EMBED_NODE: 1,
> 9	} as const;
> 10	
> 11	export type CoinOperation = keyof typeof COIN_COSTS;
> 12	
> 13	/**
> 14	 * Deducts mcoCoins from the authenticated user's profile.
> 15	 * Calls the `deduct_mco_coins` SECURITY DEFINER RPC which enforces
> 16	 * the balance check atomically in the database.
> 17	 * Throws if balance is insufficient or the RPC fails.
> 18	 */
> 19	export async function deductMcoCoins(
> 20	  supabase: SupabaseClient,
> 21	  userId: string,
> 22	  amount: number,
> 23	): Promise<void> {
> 24	  const { error } = await supabase.rpc('deduct_mco_coins', {
> 25	    p_user_id: userId,
> 26	    p_amount: amount,
> 27	  });
> 28	
> 29	  if (error) {
> 30	    throw new Error(error.message || 'Falha ao deduzir mcoCoins');
> 31	  }
> 32	}
> 33	
> 34	/**
> 35	 * Returns the current mco_balance for the given user.
> 36	 * Returns 0 if the profile row is not found.
> 37	 */
> 38	export async function getMcoBalance(
> 39	  supabase: SupabaseClient,
> 40	  userId: string,
> 41	): Promise<number> {
> 42	  const { data, error } = await supabase
> 43	    .from('profiles')
> 44	    .select('mco_balance')
> 45	    .eq('id', userId)
> 46	    .maybeSingle();
> 47	
> 48	  if (error) throw new Error(error.message);
> 49	  return (data?.mco_balance as number | null) ?? 0;
> 50	}
> 51	
> 52	/**
> 53	 * Returns true if the user has enough mcoCoins for the operation.
> 54	 * Prefer this for UI guard checks (non-destructive read).
> 55	 */
> 56	export async function hasSufficientBalance(
> 57	  supabase: SupabaseClient,
> 58	  userId: string,
> 59	  required: number,
> 60	): Promise<boolean> {
> 61	  const balance = await getMcoBalance(supabase, userId);
> 62	  return balance >= required;
> 63	}
> 64	
> 65	/**
> 66	 * Convenience wrapper: checks balance then deducts in sequence.
> 67	 * Use this in Edge Functions where you want an explicit pre-check message.
> 68	 */
> 69	export async function checkAndDeductCoins(
> 70	  supabase: SupabaseClient,
> 71	  userId: string,
> 72	  operation: CoinOperation,
> 73	): Promise<void> {
> 74	  const cost = COIN_COSTS[operation];
> 75	  const sufficient = await hasSufficientBalance(supabase, userId, cost);
> 76	  if (!sufficient) {
> 77	    throw new Error(
> 78	      `Saldo insuficiente: operação "${operation}" custa ${cost} mcoCoins`,
> 79	    );
> 80	  }
> 81	  await deductMcoCoins(supabase, userId, cost);
> 82	}
> 83	
> ```

>
> ```
> 1	-- Vector Search Optimization & mcoCoins Billing Foundation
> 2	-- Phase Beta — 2026-04-25
> 3	
> 4	-- 1. HNSW index for O(log n) cosine similarity on mcorch_nodes
> 5	--    (embedding column vector(768) already exists from mcorch_graphrag_core migration)
> 6	CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS mcorch_nodes_embedding_hnsw
> 7	  ON public.mcorch_nodes
> 8	  USING hnsw (embedding vector_cosine_ops)
> 9	  WITH (m = 16, ef_construction = 64);
> 10	
> 11	-- 2. Atomic mcoCoins deduction — SECURITY DEFINER allows the client JWT to call this safely.
> 12	--    Raises an exception (caught by supabase.rpc error) if balance is insufficient.
> 13	CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.deduct_mco_coins(
> 14	  p_user_id uuid,
> 15	  p_amount   decimal
> 16	)
> 17	RETURNS void
> 18	LANGUAGE plpgsql
> 19	SECURITY DEFINER
> 20	SET search_path = public
> 21	AS $$
> 22	BEGIN
> 23	  UPDATE profiles
> 24	     SET mco_balance = GREATEST(0, mco_balance - p_amount)
> 25	   WHERE id = p_user_id
> 26	     AND mco_balance >= p_amount;
> 27	
> 28	  IF NOT FOUND THEN
> 29	    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Saldo mcoCoins insuficiente para completar a operação';
> 30	  END IF;
> 31	END;
> 32	$$;
> 33	
> 34	-- 3. Grant execute to authenticated role so supabase.rpc() works from the client
> 35	GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.deduct_mco_coins(uuid, decimal) TO authenticated;
> 36	
> ```

>
> ```
> 1	import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
> 2	import { supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';
> 3	
> 4	export interface McorchNode {
> 5	  id: string;
> 6	  node_type: string;
> 7	  name: string;
> 8	  content: string;
> 9	  metadata: any;
> 10	  created_at: string;
> 11	}
> 12	
> 13	export interface McorchEdge {
> 14	  id: string;
> 15	  source_id: string;
> 16	  target_id: string;
> 17	  relation_type: string;
> 18	  weight: number;
> 19	  metadata: any;
> 20	}
> 21	
> 22	export const useMcorchMemory = (projectId?: string) => {
> 23	  const [nodes, setNodes] = useState<McorchNode[]>([]);
> 24	  const [edges, setEdges] = useState<McorchEdge[]>([]);
> 25	  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
> 26	  const [error, setError] = useState<Error | null>(null);
> 27	
> 28	  useEffect(() => {
> 29	    const fetchInitialGraph = async () => {
> 30	      try {
> 31	        setLoading(true);
> 32	        const { data: nodesData, error: nodesError } = await supabase
> 33	          .from('mcorch_nodes')
> 34	          .select('*');
> 35	        
> 36	        const { data: edgesData, error: edgesError } = await supabase
> 37	          .from('mcorch_edges')
> 38	          .select('*');
> 39	
> 40	        if (nodesError) throw nodesError;
> 41	        if (edgesError) throw edgesError;
> 42	
> 43	        setNodes(nodesData || []);
> 44	        setEdges(edgesData || []);
> 45	      } catch (err: any) {
> 46	        console.error('Error fetching MCORCH memory:', err);
> 47	        setError(err);
> 48	      } finally {
> 49	        setLoading(false);
> 50	      }
> 51	    };
> 52	
> 53	    fetchInitialGraph();
> 54	
> 55	    // Escuta em tempo real para novas observações capturadas pelo claude-mem
> 56	    const nodesChannel = supabase
> 57	      .channel('mcorch-nodes-changes')
> 58	      .on('postgres_changes', 
> 59	        { event: 'INSERT', schema: 'public', table: 'mcorch_nodes' }, 
> 60	        (payload) => {
> 61	          setNodes((prev) => [...prev, payload.new as McorchNode]);
> 62	        }
> 63	      )
> 64	      .subscribe();
> 65	
> 66	    const edgesChannel = supabase
> 67	      .channel('mcorch-edges-changes')
> 68	      .on('postgres_changes', 
> 69	        { event: 'INSERT', schema: 'public', table: 'mcorch_edges' }, 
> 70	        (payload) => {
> 71	          setEdges((prev) => [...prev, payload.new as McorchEdge]);
> 72	        }
> 73	      )
> 74	      .subscribe();
> 75	
> 76	    return () => {
> 77	      supabase.removeChannel(nodesChannel);
> 78	      supabase.removeChannel(edgesChannel);
> 79	    };
> 80	  }, [projectId]);
> 81	
> 82	  return { nodes, edges, loading, error };
> 83	};
> 84	
> ```

> 125	          </div>
> 126	        </CardContent>
> 127	      </Card>
> 128	    </motion.div>
> 129	  );
> 130	}
> 131	
> 132	function formatDate(dateStr: string) {
> 133	  const d = new Date(dateStr);
> 134	  const now = new Date();
> 135	  const diff = now.getTime() - d.getTime();
> 136	  const mins = Math.floor(diff / 60000);
> 137	  if (mins < 60) return `${mins}min atrás`;
> 138	  const hours = Math.floor(mins / 60);
> 139	  if (hours < 24) return `${hours}h atrás`;
> 140	  return d.toLocaleDateString('pt-BR', { day: '2-digit', month: 'short' });
> 141	}
> 142	
> 143	const IMPACT_COLORS: Record<string, string> = {
> 144	  high: 'text-green-400 bg-green-400/10',
> 145	  medium: 'text-amber-400 bg-amber-400/10',
> 146	  low: 'text-muted-foreground bg-muted/50',
> 147	};
> 148	const CATEGORY_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
> 149	  schedule: '📅 Horários',
> 150	  platform: '📱 Plataformas',
> 151	  content: '📝 Conteúdo',
> 152	  budget: '💰 Orçamento',
> 153	};
> 154	
> 155	interface OptimizationResult {
> 156	  score: number;
> 157	  summary: string;
> 158	  recommendations: Array<{ category: string; title: string; description: string; impact: string; action: string }>;
> 159	  best_times: Array<{ day: string; hour: string; platform: string }>;
> 160	  top_platforms: string[];
> 161	  content_mix: Array<{ type: string; percentage: number }>;
> 162	}
> 163	
> 164	interface HealingResult {
> 165	  total_issues: number;
> 166	  resolved: number;
> 167	  needs_attention: number;
> 168	  actions: Array<{
> 169	    issue_type: string;
> 170	    issue_details: string;
> 171	    action_taken: string;
> 172	    success: boolean;
> 173	    related_resource_type?: string;
> 174	  }>;
> 175	}
> 176	
> 177	interface OptimizationHistoryItem {
> 178	  id: string;
> 179	  action_type: string;
> 180	  action_details: any;
> 181	  context: any;
> 182	  applied: boolean;
> 183	  created_at: string;
> 184	}
> 185	
> 186	export default function DashboardHome() {
> 187	  const { user } = useAuth();
> 188	  const {
> 189	    credits,
> 190	    subscription,
> 191	    totalPosts,
> 192	    totalCampaigns,
> 193	    socialAccounts,
> 194	    recentNotifications,
> 195	    recentActivity,
> 196	    profile,
> 197	    loading,
> 198	  } = useDashboardData();
> 199	
> 200	  // Onboarding
> 201	  const [showOnboarding, setShowOnboarding] = useState(false);
> 202	  const [onboardingChecked, setOnboardingChecked] = useState(false);
> 203	
> 204	  useEffect(() => {
> 205	    if (!user || onboardingChecked) return;
> 206	    supabase.from('profiles').select('onboarding_completed').eq('id', user.id).single()
> 207	      .then(({ data }) => {
> 208	        if (data && !data.onboarding_completed) setShowOnboarding(true);
> 209	        setOnboardingChecked(true);
> 210	      });
> 211	  }, [user, onboardingChecked]);
> 212	
> 213	  const now = new Date();
> 214	  const { data: upcomingPosts } = useScheduledPosts({ from: startOfDay(now), to: endOfDay(addDays(now, 7)) });
> 215	
> 216	  const [optimizing, setOptimizing] = useState(false);
> 217	  const [optResult, setOptResult] = useState<OptimizationResult | null>(null);
> 218	  const [healing, setHealing] = useState(false);
> 219	  const [healResult, setHealResult] = useState<HealingResult | null>(null);
> 220	  const [optHistory, setOptHistory] = useState<OptimizationHistoryItem[]>([]);
> 221	  const [contentItems, setContentItems] = useState<ContentLibraryItem[]>([]);
> 222	  const [loadingContent, setLoadingContent] = useState(true);
> 223	
> 224	  // Fetch content library items and setup realtime
> 225	  useEffect(() => {
> 226	    if (!user) return;
> 227	
> 228	    const fetchContent = async () => {
> 229	      setLoadingContent(true);
> 230	      try {
> 231	        const { data, error } = await supabase
> 232	          .from('content_library')
> 233	          .select('*')
> 234	          .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
> 235	          .limit(6);
> 236	
> 237	        if (error) throw error;
> 238	
> 239	        const mappedItems: ContentLibraryItem[] = (data || []).map(item => ({
> 240	          id: item.id,
> 241	          title: item.title || 'Sem título',
> 242	          content_html: item.body || '',
> 243	          category: (item.metadata as any)?.category || item.type || 'Geral',
> 244	          image_url: item.media_url || item.thumbnail_url || '',
> 245	          created_at: item.created_at
> 246	        }));
> 247	
> 248	        setContentItems(mappedItems);
> 249	      } catch (err) {
> 250	        console.error('Erro ao carregar biblioteca:', err);
> 251	      } finally {
> 252	        setLoadingContent(false);
> 253	      }
> 254	    };
> 255	
> 256	    fetchContent();
> 257	
> 258	    // Supabase Realtime Subscription
> 259	    const channel = supabase
> 260	      .channel('public:content_library')
> 261	      .on(
> 262	        'postgres_changes',
> 263	        { event: 'INSERT', schema: 'public', table: 'content_library' },
> 264	        (payload) => {
> 265	          const newItem = payload.new as any;
> 266	          const mapped: ContentLibraryItem = {
> 267	            id: newItem.id,
> 268	            title: newItem.title || 'Novo Item',
> 269	            content_html: newItem.body || '',
> 270	            category: (newItem.metadata as any)?.category || newItem.type || 'Geral',
> 271	            image_url: newItem.media_url || newItem.thumbnail_url || '',
> 272	            created_at: newItem.created_at
> 273	          };
> 274	          
> 275	          setContentItems(prev => [mapped, ...prev].slice(0, 10));
> 276	          toast.success('Novo conteúdo injetado via n8n!');
> 277	        }
> 278	      )
> 279	      .subscribe();
> 280	
> 281	    return () => {
> 282	      supabase.removeChannel(channel);
> 283	    };
> 284	  }, [user]);
> 285	
> 286	  // Fetch optimization history
> 287	  useEffect(() => {
> 288	    if (!user) return;
> 289	    supabase
> 290	      .from('optimization_policy')
> 291	      .select('id, action_type, action_details, context, applied, created_at')
> 292	      .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
> 293	      .limit(20)
> 294	      .then(({ data }) => {
> 295	        if (data) setOptHistory(data as OptimizationHistoryItem[]);
> 296	      });
> 297	  }, [user, optResult]); // refetch after new optimization
> 298	
> 299	  const planLabel = subscription?.plan
> 300	    ? subscription.plan.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + subscription.plan.slice(1)
> 301	    : 'Free';
> 302	
> 303	  const statusBadge: Record<string, string> = {
> 304	    active: 'bg-status-active/20 text-green-400',
> 305	    trialing: 'bg-status-waiting/20 text-amber-400',
> 306	    past_due: 'bg-destructive/20 text-destructive',
> 307	    canceled: 'bg-muted text-muted-foreground',
> 308	  };
> 309	
> 310	  const runOptimization = async () => {
> 311	    setOptimizing(true);
> 312	    try {
> 313	      const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
> 314	      const { data, error } = await supabase.functions.invoke('self-optimize', {
> 315	        headers: {
> 316	          'X-User-Token': session?.access_token || '',
> 317	        }
> 318	      });
> 319	      if (error) throw error;
> 320	      setOptResult(data as OptimizationResult);
> 321	      toast.success('Análise concluída!');
> 322	    } catch (e: any) {
> 323	      toast.error(e.message || 'Erro ao otimizar');
> 324	    } finally {
> 325	      setOptimizing(false);
> 326	    }
> 327	  };
> 328	
> 329	  const runHealing = async () => {
> 330	    setHealing(true);
> 331	    try {
> 332	      const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
> 333	      const { data, error } = await supabase.functions.invoke('self-heal', {
> 334	        headers: {
> 335	          'X-User-Token': session?.access_token || '',
> 336	        }
> 337	      });
> 338	      if (error) throw error;
> 339	      setHealResult(data as HealingResult);
> 340	      if ((data as HealingResult).total_issues === 0) {
> 341	        toast.success('Nenhum problema encontrado! Tudo funcionando.');
> 342	      } else {
> 343	        toast.success(`Diagnóstico concluído: ${(data as HealingResult).resolved} resolvidos, ${(data as HealingResult).needs_attention} pendentes`);
> 344	      }
> 345	    } catch (e: any) {
> 346	      toast.error(e.message || 'Erro no diagnóstico');
> 347	    } finally {
> 348	      setHealing(false);
> 349	    }
> 350	  };
> 351	
> 352	  if (loading) {
> 353	    return (
> 354	      <div className="space-y-6">
> 355	        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
> 356	          {[...Array(4)].map((_, i) => (
> 357	            <Skeleton key={i} className="h-24 rounded-xl" />
> 358	          ))}
> 359	        </div>
> 360	        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
> 361	          <Skeleton className="h-72 rounded-xl" />
> 362	          <Skeleton className="h-72 rounded-xl" />
> 363	        </div>
> 364	      </div>
> 365	    );
> 366	  }
> 367	
> 368	  return (
> 369	    <div className="space-y-6">
> 370	      {showOnboarding && user && (
> 371	        <OnboardingWizard userId={user.id} onComplete={() => setShowOnboarding(false)} />
> 372	      )}
> 373	      {/* Header */}
> 374	      <div>
> 375	        <h2 className="font-heading text-2xl font-bold text-foreground">Visão Geral</h2>
> 376	        <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground mt-1">
> 377	          Plano {planLabel}{' '}
> 378	          <Badge variant="outline" className={statusBadge[subscription?.status || 'trialing'] || ''}>
> 379	            {subscription?.status === 'trialing' ? 'Trial' : subscription?.status || 'trial'}
> 380	          </Badge>
> 381	        </p>
> 382	      </div>
> 383	
> 384	      {/* KPIs */}
> 385	      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
> 386	        <KpiCard icon={Zap} label="Créditos de IA" value={credits?.balance ?? 0} sub={`${credits?.lifetime_spent ?? 0} gastos no total`} color="bg-primary/15 text-primary" delay={0} />
> 387	        <KpiCard icon={CalendarCheck} label="Posts Agendados" value={totalPosts} color="bg-accent/15 text-accent" delay={0.08} />
> 388	        <KpiCard icon={Megaphone} label="Campanhas" value={totalCampaigns} color="bg-status-waiting/15 text-amber-400" delay={0.16} />
> 389	        <KpiCard icon={Share2} label="Redes Conectadas" value={socialAccounts} sub="de 8 disponíveis" color="bg-status-active/15 text-green-400" delay={0.24} />
> 390	      </div>
> 391	
> 392	      {/* Wallet Widget */}
> 393	      <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }} transition={{ delay: 0.26 }}>
> 394	        <Card className="glass-panel border-primary/30 bg-gradient-to-r from-primary/10 via-background to-accent/10">
> 395	          <CardContent className="p-6">
> 396	            <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-6">
> 397	              <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
> 398	                <div className="p-3 rounded-full bg-primary/20 border border-primary/30">
> 399	                  <Wallet className="h-6 w-6 text-primary" />
> 400	                </div>
> 401	                <div>
> 402	                  <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-widest font-bold">Autonomic Wallet</p>
> 403	                  <h3 className="text-2xl font-heading font-bold text-foreground flex items-center gap-2">
> 404	                    {profile?.mco_balance || 0}
> 405	                    <span className="text-primary text-sm tracking-normal">mcoCoins</span>
> 406	                  </h3>
> 407	                </div>
> 408	              </div>
> 409	              
> 410	              <div className="h-12 w-px bg-border hidden md:block" />
> 411	              
> 412	              <div className="flex items-center gap-4 flex-1 max-w-xs">
> 413	                <div className="flex-1">
> 414	                  <div className="flex justify-between text-xs mb-1">
> 415	                    <span className="text-muted-foreground font-medium flex items-center gap-1">
> 416	                      <Trophy className="h-3 w-3 text-accent" />
> 417	                      Score de Engajamento
> 418	                    </span>
> 419	                    <span className="text-foreground font-bold">{profile?.score || 0} pts</span>
> 420	                  </div>
> 421	                  <Progress value={(profile?.score || 0) % 100} className="h-1.5 bg-accent/10" />
> 422	                  <p className="text-[10px] text-muted-foreground mt-1 text-right italic">
> 423	                    {100 - ((profile?.score || 0) % 100)} pts para a próxima mcoCoin
> 424	                  </p>
> 425	                </div>
> 426	              </div>
> 427	
> 428	              <div className="flex gap-2">
> 429	                <Button size="sm" variant="outline" className="text-xs border-primary/20 hover:bg-primary/10">
> 430	                  <Coins className="h-3.5 w-3.5 mr-2 text-primary" />
> 431	                  Trocar
> 432	                </Button>
> 433	                <Button size="sm" className="text-xs bg-primary/20 text-primary hover:bg-primary/30 border-none">
> 434	                  Fazer Upgrade
> 435	                </Button>
> 436	              </div>
> 437	            </div>
> 438	          </CardContent>
> 439	        </Card>
> 440	      </motion.div>
> 441	
> 442	      {/* Self-Optimization + Self-Healing row */}
> 443	      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
> 444	        {/* Self-Optimization Widget */}
> 445	        <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }} transition={{ delay: 0.28 }}>
> 446	          <Card className="glass-panel border-primary/20 h-full">
> 447	            <CardHeader className="pb-2">
> 448	              <CardTitle className="font-heading text-base flex items-center gap-2">
> 449	                <Brain className="h-4 w-4 text-primary" />
> 450	                Auto-otimização IA
> 451	                <Badge variant="outline" className="ml-auto text-[10px]">Fase 16</Badge>
> 452	              </CardTitle>
> 453	            </CardHeader>
> 454	            <CardContent>
> 455	              {!optResult ? (
> 456	                <div className="flex flex-col items-center py-6 gap-4">
> 457	                  <Sparkles className="h-10 w-10 text-primary/60" />
> 458	                  <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground text-center max-w-md">
> 459	                    Analise suas métricas com IA para recomendações personalizadas.
> 460	                  </p>
> 461	                  <Button onClick={runOptimization} disabled={optimizing} className="gap-2">
> 462	                    {optimizing ? <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> : <Brain className="h-4 w-4" />}
> 463	                    {optimizing ? 'Analisando...' : 'Analisar e Otimizar'}
> 464	                  </Button>
> 465	                </div>
> 466	              ) : (
> 467	                <div className="space-y-4">
> 468	                  <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
> 469	                    <div className="text-center">
> 470	                      <p className="text-3xl font-bold text-primary">{optResult.score}</p>
> 471	                      <p className="text-[10px] text-muted-foreground">Score</p>
> 472	                    </div>
> 473	                    <div className="flex-1">
> 474	                      <Progress value={optResult.score} className="h-2" />
> 475	                      <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-1">{optResult.summary}</p>
> 476	                    </div>
> 477	                    <Button variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={runOptimization} disabled={optimizing} className="gap-1">
> 478	                      {optimizing ? <Loader2 className="h-3 w-3 animate-spin" /> : <ArrowUpRight className="h-3 w-3" />}
> 479	                      Reanalisar
> 480	                    </Button>
> 481	                  </div>
> 482	                  <div className="space-y-2">
> 483	                    <p className="text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-wider">Recomendações</p>
> 484	                    {optResult.recommendations.slice(0, 4).map((rec, i) => (
> 485	                      <div key={i} className="flex items-start gap-3 p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-secondary/30 text-sm">
> 486	                        <span className="text-xs mt-0.5">{CATEGORY_LABELS[rec.category] || rec.category}</span>
> 487	                        <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
> 488	                          <p className="font-medium text-foreground text-xs">{rec.title}</p>
> 489	                          <p className="text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">{rec.description}</p>
> 490	                        </div>
> 491	                        <Badge variant="outline" className={`text-[10px] ${IMPACT_COLORS[rec.impact] || ''}`}>
> 492	                          {rec.impact}
> 493	                        </Badge>
> 494	                      </div>
> 495	                    ))}
> 496	                  </div>
> 497	                </div>
> 498	              )}
> 499	            </CardContent>
> 500	          </Card>
> 501	        </motion.div>
> 502	
> 503	        {/* Self-Healing Widget */}
> 504	        <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }} transition={{ delay: 0.32 }}>
> 505	          <Card className="glass-panel border-accent/20 h-full">
> 506	            <CardHeader className="pb-2">
> 507	              <CardTitle className="font-heading text-base flex items-center gap-2">
> 508	                <HeartPulse className="h-4 w-4 text-accent" />
> 509	                Self-Healing
> 510	                <Badge variant="outline" className="ml-auto text-[10px]">Fase 17</Badge>
> 511	              </CardTitle>
> 512	            </CardHeader>
> 513	            <CardContent>
> 514	              {!healResult ? (
> 515	                <div className="flex flex-col items-center py-6 gap-4">
> 516	                  <Shield className="h-10 w-10 text-accent/60" />
> 517	                  <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground text-center max-w-md">
> 518	                    Detecta posts falhos, tokens expirados e publicações travadas — corrige automaticamente.
> 519	                  </p>
> 520	                  <Button onClick={runHealing} disabled={healing} variant="outline" className="gap-2 border-accent/30 hover:bg-accent/10">
> 521	                    {healing ? <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> : <HeartPulse className="h-4 w-4" />}
> 522	                    {healing ? 'Diagnosticando...' : 'Executar Diagnóstico'}
> 523	                  </Button>
> 524	                </div>
> 525	              ) : (
> 526	                <div className="space-y-4">
> 527	                  {/* Summary */}
> 528	                  <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
> 529	                    <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-3 flex-1">
> 530	                      <div className="text-center p-2 rounded-lg bg-secondary/30">
> 531	                        <p className="text-xl font-bold text-foreground">{healResult.total_issues}</p>
> 532	                        <p className="text-[10px] text-muted-foreground">Problemas</p>
> 533	                      </div>
> 534	                      <div className="text-center p-2 rounded-lg bg-green-500/10">
> 535	                        <p className="text-xl font-bold text-green-400">{healResult.resolved}</p>
> 536	                        <p className="text-[10px] text-muted-foreground">Resolvidos</p>
> 537	                      </div>
> 538	                      <div className="text-center p-2 rounded-lg bg-destructive/10">
> 539	                        <p className="text-xl font-bold text-destructive">{healResult.needs_attention}</p>
> 540	                        <p className="text-[10px] text-muted-foreground">Pendentes</p>
> 541	                      </div>
> 542	                    </div>
> 543	                    <Button variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={runHealing} disabled={healing} className="gap-1">
> 544	                      {healing ? <Loader2 className="h-3 w-3 animate-spin" /> : <RefreshCw className="h-3 w-3" />}
> 545	                      Rediagnosticar
> 546	                    </Button>
> 547	                  </div>
> 548	
> 549	                  {/* Actions */}
> 550	                  {healResult.total_issues === 0 ? (
> 551	                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 p-3 rounded-lg bg-green-500/10 text-sm">
> 552	                      <CheckCircle2 className="h-4 w-4 text-green-400" />
> 553	                      <span className="text-green-400 font-medium">Tudo funcionando corretamente!</span>
> 554	                    </div>
> 555	                  ) : (
> 556	                    <div className="space-y-2 max-h-48 overflow-y-auto">
> 557	                      {healResult.actions.map((action, i) => (
> 558	                        <div key={i} className="flex items-start gap-2 p-2 rounded-lg bg-secondary/30 text-xs">
> 559	                          {action.success ? (
> 560	                            <CheckCircle2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-green-400 mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
> 561	                          ) : (
> 562	                            <XCircle className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-destructive mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
> 563	                          )}
> 564	                          <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
> 565	                            <p className="font-medium text-foreground">{action.issue_details}</p>
> 566	                            <p className="text-muted-foreground">{action.action_taken}</p>
> 567	                          </div>
> 568	                        </div>
> 569	                      ))}
> 570	                    </div>
> 571	                  )}
> 572	                </div>
> 573	              )}
> 574	            </CardContent>
> 575	          </Card>
> 576	        </motion.div>
> 577	      </div>
> 578	
> 579	      {/* Optimization History */}
> 580	      {optHistory.length > 0 && (
> 581	        <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }} transition={{ delay: 0.36 }}>
> 582	          <Card className="glass-panel">
> 583	            <CardHeader className="pb-2">
> 584	              <CardTitle className="font-heading text-base flex items-center gap-2">
> 585	                <History className="h-4 w-4 text-primary" />
> 586	                Histórico de Otimizações
> 587	                <Badge variant="outline" className="ml-auto text-[10px]">{optHistory.length} registros</Badge>
> 588	              </CardTitle>
> 589	            </CardHeader>
> 590	            <CardContent>
> 591	              <div className="space-y-2 max-h-64 overflow-y-auto">
> 592	                {optHistory.map((item) => (
> 593	                  <div key={item.id} className="flex items-start gap-3 p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-secondary/30 text-sm">
> 594	                    <span className="text-xs mt-0.5">{CATEGORY_LABELS[item.action_type] || item.action_type}</span>
> 595	                    <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
> 596	                      <p className="font-medium text-foreground text-xs">{item.action_details?.title || item.action_type}</p>
> 597	                      <p className="text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">{item.action_details?.description || ''}</p>
> 598	                    </div>
> 599	                    <div className="flex flex-col items-end gap-1 shrink-0">
> 600	                      <Badge
> 601	                        variant="outline"
> 602	                        className={`text-[10px] ${item.applied ? 'text-green-400 bg-green-400/10' : 'text-muted-foreground bg-muted/50'}`}
> 603	                      >
> 604	                        {item.applied ? 'Aplicado' : 'Pendente'}
> 605	                      </Badge>
> 606	                      <span className="text-[10px] text-muted-foreground">
> 607	                        {format(parseISO(item.created_at), "dd/MM HH:mm", { locale: ptBR })}

> 114	
> 115	    let tick = 0;
> 116	
> 117	    function step() {
> 118	      simulation.tick();
> 119	      tick++;
> 120	
> 121	      for (const n of simNodes) {
> 122	        positionsRef.current.set(n.id, {
> 123	          x: Math.max(12, Math.min(WIDTH - 12, n.x ?? WIDTH / 2)),
> 124	          y: Math.max(12, Math.min(HEIGHT - 12, n.y ?? HEIGHT / 2)),
> 125	        });
> 126	      }
> 127	
> 128	      if (tick % 8 === 0) {
> 129	        setRenderTick((t) => t + 1);
> 130	      }
> 131	
> 132	      if (simulation.alpha() > simulation.alphaMin()) {
> 133	        rafRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(step);
> 134	      } else {
> 135	        setRenderTick((t) => t + 1);
> 136	      }
> 137	    }
> 138	
> 139	    rafRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(step);
> 140	
> 141	    return () => {
> 142	      if (rafRef.current !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(rafRef.current);
> 143	      simulation.stop();
> 144	    };
> 145	  }, [nodes, edges]);
> 146	
> 147	  if (loading) {
> 148	    return (
> 149	      <Card className="glass-panel overflow-hidden">
> 150	        <CardContent className="h-[320px] flex items-center justify-center">
> 151	          <Loader2 className="h-5 w-5 animate-spin text-primary" />
> 152	        </CardContent>
> 153	      </Card>
> 154	    );
> 155	  }
> 156	
> 157	  if (error) {
> 158	    return (
> 159	      <Card className="glass-panel overflow-hidden">
> 160	        <CardContent className="h-[320px] flex items-center justify-center text-destructive text-sm">
> 161	          Erro ao carregar Knowledge Mesh
> 162	        </CardContent>
> 163	      </Card>
> 164	    );
> 165	  }
> 166	
> 167	  return (
> 168	    <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }} transition={{ delay: 0.4 }}>
> 169	      <Card className="glass-panel overflow-hidden">
> 170	        <CardHeader className="pb-2 flex flex-row items-center justify-between">
> 171	          <CardTitle className="font-heading text-base flex items-center gap-2">
> 172	            <Brain className="h-4 w-4 text-primary" />
> 173	            Knowledge Mesh
> 174	            <Badge variant="outline" className="text-xs ml-1 border-primary/40 text-primary">
> 175	              {nodes.length} nós · {edges.length} arestas
> 176	            </Badge>
> 177	          </CardTitle>
> 178	          <RefreshCw className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-muted-foreground" />
> 179	        </CardHeader>
> 180	        <CardContent className="p-0 relative" style={{ minHeight: HEIGHT }}>
> 181	          <svg
> 182	            width="100%"
> 183	            viewBox={`0 0 ${WIDTH} ${HEIGHT}`}
> 184	            className="w-full"
> 185	            style={{ background: 'transparent' }}
> 186	          >
> 187	            {/* Edges — read positions from ref */}
> 188	            {edges.map((edge) => {
> 189	              const sp = positionsRef.current.get(edge.source_id);
> 190	              const tp = positionsRef.current.get(edge.target_id);
> 191	              if (!sp || !tp) return null;
> 192	              return (
> 193	                <line
> 194	                  key={edge.id}
> 195	                  x1={sp.x}
> 196	                  y1={sp.y}
> 197	                  x2={tp.x}
> 198	                  y2={tp.y}
> 199	                  stroke="#38bdf8"
> 200	                  strokeOpacity={0.22}
> 201	                  strokeWidth={1}
> 202	                />
> 203	              );
> 204	            })}
> 205	
> 206	            {/* Nodes */}
> 207	            {nodes.map((node, i) => {
> 208	              const pos = positionsRef.current.get(node.id) ?? { x: WIDTH / 2, y: HEIGHT / 2 };
> 209	              const color = NODE_TYPE_COLOR[node.node_type] ?? NODE_TYPE_COLOR.default;
> 210	              return (
> 211	                <g
> 212	                  key={node.id}
> 213	                  style={{ cursor: 'pointer' }}
> 214	                  onMouseEnter={() => setTooltip({ node })}
> 215	                  onMouseLeave={() => setTooltip(null)}
> 216	                >
> 217	                  <motion.circle
> 218	                    cx={pos.x}
> 219	                    cy={pos.y}
> 220	                    r={10}
> 221	                    fill={color}
> 222	                    fillOpacity={0.15}
> 223	                    stroke={color}
> 224	                    strokeWidth={1.5}
> 225	                    initial={{ scale: 0, opacity: 0 }}
> 226	                    animate={{ scale: 1, opacity: 1 }}
> 227	                    transition={{ delay: i * 0.04, type: 'spring', stiffness: 220 }}
> 228	                    whileHover={{ scale: 1.45, fillOpacity: 0.4 }}
> 229	                  />
> 230	                  <motion.text
> 231	                    x={pos.x}
> 232	                    y={pos.y + 20}
> 233	                    textAnchor="middle"
> 234	                    fontSize={7}
> 235	                    fill={color}
> 236	                    fillOpacity={0.8}
> 237	                    initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
> 238	                    animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
> 239	                    transition={{ delay: i * 0.04 + 0.25 }}
> 240	                  >
> 241	                    {node.name.length > 14 ? node.name.slice(0, 14) + '…' : node.name}
> 242	                  </motion.text>
> 243	                </g>
> 244	              );
> 245	            })}
> 246	
> 247	            {nodes.length === 0 && (
> 248	              <text x={WIDTH / 2} y={HEIGHT / 2} textAnchor="middle" fontSize={11} fill="#64748b">
> 249	                Knowledge Mesh vazio
> 250	              </text>
> 251	            )}
> 252	          </svg>
> 253	
> 254	          {/* Tooltip overlay */}
> 255	          <AnimatePresence>
> 256	            {tooltip && (
> 257	              <motion.div
> 258	                initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.92 }}
> 259	                animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1 }}
> 260	                exit={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.92 }}
> 261	                className="absolute bottom-2 left-2 right-2 z-10 glass-panel rounded-lg p-3 border border-primary/30 text-xs"
> 262	              >
> 263	                <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
> 264	                  <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
> 265	                    <p className="font-semibold text-foreground truncate">{tooltip.node.name}</p>
> 266	                    <p className="text-muted-foreground mt-0.5 line-clamp-2">{tooltip.node.content}</p>
> 267	                  </div>
> 268	                  <Badge
> 269	                    variant="outline"
> 270	                    className="shrink-0 text-[10px]"
> 271	                    style={{
> 272	                      borderColor: NODE_TYPE_COLOR[tooltip.node.node_type] ?? NODE_TYPE_COLOR.default,
> 273	                      color: NODE_TYPE_COLOR[tooltip.node.node_type] ?? NODE_TYPE_COLOR.default,
> 274	                    }}
> 275	                  >
> 276	                    {tooltip.node.node_type}
> 277	                  </Badge>
> 278	                </div>
> 279	              </motion.div>
> 280	            )}
> 281	          </AnimatePresence>
> 282	        </CardContent>
> 283	      </Card>
> 284	    </motion.div>
> 285	  );
> 286	}
> 287	
> ```

Decay function \u2014 called by pg_cron daily.\n--    Formula: new_stability = stability * exp(-days_since_seen / halflife)\n--    Half-life = 30 days (configurable). Minimum floor = 0.05.\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.decay_mcorch_stability()\nRETURNS void\nLANGUAGE plpgsql\nSECURITY DEFINER\nSET search_path = public\nAS $$\nDECLARE\n  halflife_days CONSTANT float := 30.0;\nBEGIN\n  UPDATE public.mcorch_nodes\n  SET stability_score = GREATEST(\n    0.05,\n    stability_score * exp(\n      -EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (now() - COALESCE(last_seen_at, created_at))) / 86400.0 / halflife_days\n    )\n  );\nEND;\n$$;\n\n-- 3. Schedule daily decay via pg_cron (runs at 03:00 UTC)\n--    Requires pg_cron extension \u2014 enabled on Supabase Pro+.\n--    On free tier: call manually or via Supabase Edge Function cron trigger.\nSELECT cron.schedule(\n  'mcorch-stability-decay',\n  '0 3 * * *',\n  $$ SELECT public.decay_mcorch_stability(); $$\n) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING;\n\n-- 4. Grant execute to service_role for manual invocation\nGRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.decay_mcorch_stability() TO service_role;\n"
}

```

{
  "file_path": "/home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/src/hooks/useMcorchMemory.ts",
  "content": "import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';\nimport { supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';\n\nexport interface McorchNode {\n  id: string;\n  node_type: string;\n  name: string;\n  content: string;\n  metadata: any;\n  stability_score: number;\n  last_seen_at: string | null;\n  created_at: string;\n}\n\nexport interface McorchEdge {\n  id: string;\n  source_id: string;\n  target_id: string;\n  relation_type: string;\n  weight: number;\n  metadata: any;\n}\n\nexport interface CreateNodePayload {\n  node_type: string;\n  name: string;\n  content: string;\n  metadata?: Record<string, unknown>;\n  project_id?: string;\n}\n\nexport const useMcorchMemory = (projectId?: string) => {\n  const [nodes, setNodes] = useState<McorchNode[]>([]);\n  const [edges, setEdges] = useState<McorchEdge[]>([]);\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);\n  const [error, setError] = useState<Error | null>(null);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const fetchInitialGraph = async () => {\n      try {\n        setLoading(true);\n        const { data: nodesData, error: nodesError } = await supabase\n          .from('mcorch_nodes')\n          .select('*');\n\n        const { data: edgesData, error: edgesError } = await supabase\n          .from('mcorch_edges')\n          .select('*');\n\n        if (nodesError) throw nodesError;\n        if (edgesError) throw edgesError;\n\n        setNodes(nodesData || []);\n        setEdges(edgesData || []);\n      } catch (err: any) {\n        console.error('Error fetching MCORCH memory:', err);\n        setError(err);\n      } finally {\n        setLoading(false);\n      }\n    };\n\n    fetchInitialGraph();\n\n    const nodesChannel = supabase\n      .channel('mcorch-nodes-changes')\n      .on('postgres_changes',\n        { event: 'INSERT', schema: 'public', table: 'mcorch_nodes' },\n        (payload) => {\n          setNodes((prev) => [...prev, payload.new as McorchNode]);\n        }\n      )\n      .subscribe();\n\n    const edgesChannel = supabase\n      .channel('mcorch-edges-changes')\n      .on('postgres_changes',\n        { event: 'INSERT', schema: 'public', table: 'mcorch_edges' },\n        (payload) => {\n          setEdges((prev) => [...prev, payload.new as McorchEdge]);\n        }\n      )\n      .subscribe();\n\n    return () => {\n      supabase.removeChannel(nodesChannel);\n      supabase.removeChannel(edgesChannel);\n    };\n  }, [projectId]);\n\n  /**\n   * Inserts a new node and fires the embed-mcorch-node Edge Function\n   * so the vector is generated immediately. Non-blocking: embedding\n   * failure is logged but does not reject the creation.\n   */\n  const createNode = async (payload: CreateNodePayload): Promise<McorchNode> => {\n    const { data, error: insertErr } = await supabase\n      .from('mcorch_nodes')\n      .insert({ ...payload, stability_score: 1.0 })\n      .select()\n      .single();\n\n    if (insertErr || !data) throw insertErr ?? new Error('Node insert returned no data');\n\n    const node = data as McorchNode;\n\n    // Trigger embedding asynchronously \u2014 ignore error, will retry on next backfill\n    supabase.functions\n      .invoke('embed-mcorch-node', { body: { node_id: node.id } })\n      .catch((e) => console.warn('embed-mcorch-node dispatch failed:', e));\n\n    return node;\n  };\n\n  return { nodes, edges, loading, error, createNode };\n};\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/scripts/backfill-embeddings.ts",
  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env -S deno run --allow-env --allow-net\n/**\n * Backfill embeddings for all mcorch_nodes that have embedding = NULL.\n * Usage: deno run --allow-env --allow-net scripts/backfill-embeddings.ts\n *\n * Requires env vars:\n *   SUPABASE_URL\n *   SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY\n *   SUPABASE_FUNCTIONS_URL  (e.g. https://<project-ref>.supabase.co/functions/v1)\n */\n\nconst SUPABASE_URL = Deno.env.get(\"SUPABASE_URL\");\nconst SERVICE_KEY = Deno.env.get(\"SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY\");\nconst FUNCTIONS_URL = Deno.env.get(\"SUPABASE_FUNCTIONS_URL\") ?? `${SUPABASE_URL}/functions/v1`;\n\nif (!SUPABASE_URL || !SERVICE_KEY) {\n  console.error(\"Missing SUPABASE_URL or SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY\");\n  Deno.exit(1);\n}\n\nconst headers = {\n  \"apikey\": SERVICE_KEY,\n  \"Authorization\": `Bearer ${SERVICE_KEY}`,\n  \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\",\n};\n\n// Fetch nodes with null embedding\nconst res = await fetch(\n  `${SUPABASE_URL}/rest/v1/mcorch_nodes?embedding=is.null&select=id,name`,\n  { headers },\n);\nif (!res.ok) {\n  console.error(\"Failed to fetch nodes:\", await res.text());\n  Deno.exit(1);\n}\n\nconst nodes: Array<{ id: string; name: string }> = await res.json();\nconsole.log(`Found ${nodes.length} nodes without embeddings.`);\n\nlet ok = 0;\nlet fail = 0;\n\nfor (const node of nodes) {\n  const embedRes = await fetch(`${FUNCTIONS_URL}/embed-mcorch-node`, {\n    method: \"POST\",\n    headers: { ...headers, \"apikey\": SERVICE_KEY },\n    body: JSON.stringify({ node_id: node.id }),\n  });\n\n  if (embedRes.ok) {\n    const result = await embedRes.json();\n    console.log(`\u2713 ${node.name} (${node.id}) \u2014 ${result.dims}d`);\n    ok++;\n  } else {\n    const err = await embedRes.text();\n    console.error(`\u2717 ${node.name} (${node.id}) \u2014 ${err}`);\n    fail++;\n  }\n\n  // Rate-limit: 2 embeddings per second\n  await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 500));\n}\n\nconsole.log(`\\nDone. ${ok} embedded, ${fail} failed.`);\n"
}
```

}

NODE_TYPE_COLOR.default;\n              const stability = Math.max(0.05, Math.min(1, node.stability_score ?? 1));\n              // Brighter / larger nodes have higher stability\n              const radius = 7 + stability * 5;\n              const fillOpacity = 0.08 + stability * 0.22;\n              const strokeOpacity = 0.4 + stability * 0.6;\n              return (\n                <g\n                  key={node.id}\n                  style={{ cursor: 'pointer' }}\n                  onMouseEnter={() => setTooltip({ node })}\n                  onMouseLeave={() => setTooltip(null)}\n                >\n                  <motion.circle\n                    cx={pos.x}\n                    cy={pos.y}\n                    r={radius}\n                    fill={color}\n                    fillOpacity={fillOpacity}\n                    stroke={color}\n                    strokeWidth={1.5}\n                    strokeOpacity={strokeOpacity}\n                    initial={{ scale: 0, opacity: 0 }}\n                    animate={{ scale: 1, opacity: 1 }}\n                    transition={{ delay: i * 0.04, type: 'spring', stiffness: 220 }}\n                    whileHover={{ scale: 1.45, fillOpacity: 0.4 }}\n                  />"

## 🤖 Claude *(22:19:36)*

**Phase Beta: Selada.** Commit `9fa7e80`. Aqui o resumo do que foi entregue:

---

### O que foi feito

**1. Revenue Wiring — mcoCoins Gatekeeper**
`orchestrate-content/index.ts`: antes de qualquer trabalho de IA, checa `profiles.mco_balance`. Se `< 10`, retorna HTTP 402 com `"Saldo insuficiente de mcoCoins"`. Deducão atômica via RPC `deduct_mco_coins`.

**2. Neural Activation — Auto-Embed Trigger**
`useMcorchMemory.ts`: novo método `createNode()` que insere o nó e dispara `embed-mcorch-node` automaticamente (fire-and-forget, não bloqueia a criação).

**3. Backfill Script**
`scripts/backfill-embeddings.ts`: script Deno que consulta todos os nós com `embedding = NULL` e chama a Edge Function para cada um, com rate-limit de 2 req/s.

**4. Memory Decay — FSRS-6**
`migration 20260425190000`: coluna `stability_score float DEFAULT 1.0`, função `decay_mcorch_stability()` com half-life de 30 dias, cron diário às 03:00 UTC via `pg_cron`.

**5. MemoryGraphWidget — Stability Brightness**
Nós com `stability_score` alto aparecem maiores (raio 7–12px) e mais opacos. Nós que decaem ficam menores e mais opacos.

**6. Dashboard Header HUD**
`DashboardLayout.tsx`: badge real-time `X mcoCoins` no header, alimentado por Supabase Realtime (atualiza imediatamente após dedução).

---

### Deploy Checklist (ação manual necessária)

```bash
# 1. Aplicar as 2 migrations pendentes
supabase db push

# 2. Setar secrets no vault (se ainda não estiverem)
supabase secrets set OPENROUTER_API_KEY=<key>

# 3. Deploy da Edge Function
supabase functions deploy embed-mcorch-node

# 4. Backfill dos 6 nós existentes
export SUPABASE_URL=<url>
export SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY=<key>
export SUPABASE_FUNCTIONS_URL=<url>/functions/v1
deno run --allow-env --allow-net scripts/backfill-embeddings.ts
```

Add container health polling endpoint (poll mcorch health endpoint every 30s)\n   - Add Chroma vector DB status indicator\n   - Display neural nodes count from sync-knowledge-mesh\n   - Add ROI/revenue ticker in HUD\n\n2. **ROI Widget Improvements:**\n   - Query `affiliate_links` directly (revenue_cents) instead of hardcoded estimates\n   - Add conversion rate calculation\n   - Integrate with `usage_tracking` for accurate API costs\n   - Show affiliate-specific ROI separately from campaign ROI\n\n3. **New Container Health Monitoring:**\n   - ### 1. SECURITY: MULTI-TENANT HARDENING (CRITICAL)
- **Schema Migration:** Create `20260425210000_mcorch_tenant_isolation.sql`:
    - Add `user_id (uuid)` and `project_id (text)` to `mcorch_nodes` and `mcorch_edges`.
    - Set `user_id` default to `auth.uid()`.
- **RLS Refactor:** Replace `USING (true)` with restrictive policies: `auth.uid() = user_id`.
- **Hook Update:** Refactor `src/hooks/useMcorchMemory.ts` to explicitly filter all Supabase queries using the `projectId` parameter.

### 2. DATA-DRIVEN ROI (FINANCIAL TRUTH)
- **Widget Refactor:** Update `src/components/dashboard/ROIWidget.tsx`.
- **Logic:** Replace hardcoded estimates with real data:
    - **Revenue:** Aggregate `revenue_cents` from `affiliate_links`.
    - **Costs:** Sum `spend_cents` (campaigns) + `credits_consumed` (AI tokens).
- **ROI Formula:** Implement $$ROI = \frac{Revenue - TotalCosts}{TotalCosts} \times 100$$ directly in the component.

### 3. VEO 3.1 & KNOWLEDGE SYNC
- **Function Integration:** In `supabase/functions/orchestrate-content/index.ts`:
    - After a successful video job from Veo 3.1, automatically trigger `createNode`.
    - **Node Type:** `observation`.
    - **Metadata:** `{ media_type: 'video', provider: 'google_veo', cost_mco: 15, roi_potential: 'high' }`.

### 4. COMMAND HUD (NEURAL STATUS)
- **Health Check:** Create a small Edge Function `get-infra-status` that pings the Watchdog port (37777) and Chroma (8001).
- **HUD Integration:** Add a "Neural Mesh" indicator in `src/components/HUD.tsx` using a pulsating light (Green: Healthy / Red: Unhealthy).

### 5. PRODUCTION SEAL & HANDOFF
- **Deploy:** `supabase db push` and `supabase functions deploy orchestrate-content`.
- **Sync:** Execute `/memory summarize` and update `HANDOFF.md`.
- **Commit:** `feat(gamma): tenant isolation, real-time roi, and veo-knowledge bridge`.

**DIRECTIVE:** No intros. High-effort mode. Ensure RLS doesn't lock the 'service_role' out of the backfill process.

> ```
> 1	-- Phase Alpha: GraphRAG Core Schema
> 2	-- Implementação da Memória Relacional para o Ecossistema MCORCH
> 3	
> 4	-- Habilitar extensões necessárias
> 5	CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS "vector";
> 6	CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS "uuid-ossp";
> 7	
> 8	-- Tabela de Nós (Entidades de Conhecimento)
> 9	CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.mcorch_nodes (
> 10	    id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
> 11	    node_type text NOT NULL, -- 'file', 'feature', 'decision', 'requirement', 'observation'
> 12	    name text NOT NULL,
> 13	    content text,
> 14	    metadata jsonb DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
> 15	    embedding vector(768), -- Otimizado para nomic-embed-text ou text-embedding-3-small
> 16	    created_at timestamptz DEFAULT now(),
> 17	    last_seen_at timestamptz DEFAULT now()
> 18	);
> 19	
> 20	-- Tabela de Arestas (Relacionamentos Relacionais)
> 21	CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.mcorch_edges (
> 22	    id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
> 23	    source_id uuid REFERENCES public.mcorch_nodes(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
> 24	    target_id uuid REFERENCES public.mcorch_nodes(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
> 25	    relation_type text NOT NULL, -- 'implements', 'depends_on', 'calls', 'fixes', 'supersedes'
> 26	    weight float DEFAULT 1.0,
> 27	    metadata jsonb DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
> 28	    created_at timestamptz DEFAULT now()
> 29	);
> 30	
> 31	-- Habilitar RLS
> 32	ALTER TABLE public.mcorch_nodes ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
> 33	ALTER TABLE public.mcorch_edges ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
> 34	
> 35	-- Políticas de Acesso
> 36	CREATE POLICY "Allow authenticated to view nodes" ON public.mcorch_nodes FOR SELECT TO authenticated USING (true);
> 37	CREATE POLICY "Allow service_role to manage nodes" ON public.mcorch_nodes FOR ALL TO service_role USING (true) WITH CHECK (true);
> 38	
> 39	CREATE POLICY "Allow authenticated to view edges" ON public.mcorch_edges FOR SELECT TO authenticated USING (true);
> 40	CREATE POLICY "Allow service_role to manage edges" ON public.mcorch_edges FOR ALL TO service_role USING (true) WITH CHECK (true);
> 41	
> 42	-- Função de Busca Híbrida Graph-Aware (V2)
> 43	CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.match_mcorch_memory_v2(
> 44	    query_embedding vector(768),
> 45	    match_threshold float,
> 46	    match_count int
> 47	)
> 48	RETURNS TABLE (
> 49	    id uuid,
> 50	    name text,
> 51	    content text,
> 52	    similarity float,
> 53	    relation_count bigint
> 54	)
> 55	LANGUAGE plpgsql
> 56	AS $$
> 57	BEGIN
> 58	    RETURN QUERY
> 59	    SELECT
> 60	        n.id,
> 61	        n.name,
> 62	        n.content,
> 63	        1 - (n.embedding <=> query_embedding) AS similarity,
> 64	        (SELECT count(*) FROM public.mcorch_edges e WHERE e.source_id = n.id OR e.target_id = n.id) as relation_count
> 65	    FROM public.mcorch_nodes n
> 66	    WHERE 1 - (n.embedding <=> query_embedding) > match_threshold
> 67	    ORDER BY similarity DESC, relation_count DESC
> 68	    LIMIT match_count;
> 69	END;
> 70	$$;
> 71	
> ```

> 2	# MCORCH Intelligence Node Watchdog

> 8	DATE=$(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')
> 9	COMPOSE_DIR="/home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra"
> 10	
> 11	log() { echo "[$DATE] $1" >> "$LOG_FILE"; }
> 12	
> 13	# Check if container is running

> 15	    log "ALERT: $CONTAINER not running. Starting via docker compose..."
> 16	    cd "$COMPOSE_DIR" && docker compose up -d mcorch-intelligence-node
> 17	    log "Restart attempted."
> 18	    exit 0
> 19	fi
> 20	
> 21	# Ensure lock file exists (prevents false unhealthy state)
> 22	docker exec "$CONTAINER" touch /app/system/autonomic.lock 2>/dev/null
> 23	
> 24	# Check health status
> 25	HEALTH=$(docker inspect --format='{{.State.Health.Status}}' "$CONTAINER" 2>/dev/null)
> 26	if [ "$HEALTH" = "unhealthy" ]; then
> 27	    log "ALERT: $CONTAINER is unhealthy. Restarting..."
> 28	    docker restart "$CONTAINER"
> 29	    log "Container restarted."
> 30	fi
> 31

\"http://localhost:8001/api/v2/heartbeat\" > /dev/null 2>&1; then\n  CHROMA_STATUS=\"healthy\"\nelse\n  CHROMA_STATUS=\"unhealthy\"\nfi\n\nfor SERVICE in \"mcorch_claude_mem:${HEALTH:-unknown}\" \"mcorch_chroma:${CHROMA_STATUS}\"; do\n  SVC=\"${SERVICE%%:*}\"; STS=\"${SERVICE##*:}\"\n  curl -s -X POST \"${SUPABASE_URL}/rest/v1/infra_health_logs\" \\\n    -H \"apikey:   "content": "-- Multi-Tenant Isolation for mcorch_nodes / mcorch_edges\n-- Phase Gamma \u2014 2026-04-25\n--\n-- Strategy: Add user_id + project_id to both tables.\n-- Existing rows (6 nodes, 4 edges) run under service_role context during migration,\n-- so auth.uid() = NULL \u2192 they become \"system nodes\" visible to all authenticated users.\n-- New rows inserted from the client get user_id = auth.uid() automatically via DEFAULT.\n\n-- \u2500\u2500\u2500 1. Add tenant columns \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n\nALTER TABLE public.mcorch_nodes\n  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS user_id    uuid    DEFAULT auth.uid(),\n  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS project_id text;\n\nALTER TABLE public.mcorch_edges\n  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS user_id    uuid    DEFAULT auth.uid(),\n  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS project_id text;\n\n-- \u2500\u2500\u2500 2. Drop old permissive policies \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n\nDROP POLICY IF EXISTS \"Allow authenticated to view nodes\"    ON public.mcorch_nodes;\nDROP POLICY IF EXISTS \"Allow service_role to manage nodes\"  ON public.mcorch_nodes;\nDROP POLICY IF EXISTS \"Allow authenticated to view edges\"    ON public.mcorch_edges;\nDROP POLICY IF EXISTS \"Allow service_role to manage edges\"  ON public.mcorch_edges;\n\n-- \u2500\u2500\u2500 3. mcorch_nodes \u2014 tenant-scoped policies \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n\n-- SELECT: own nodes + system nodes (user_id IS NULL = pre-migration system knowledge)\nCREATE POLICY \"nodes_tenant_select\" ON public.mcorch_nodes\n  FOR SELECT TO authenticated\n  USING (user_id IS NULL OR auth.uid() = user_id);\n\n-- INSERT: DEFAULT auth.uid() handles value; CHECK ensures no spoofing\nCREATE POLICY \"nodes_tenant_insert\" ON public.mcorch_nodes\n  FOR INSERT TO authenticated\n  WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = user_id);\n\n-- UPDATE / DELETE: own nodes only\nCREATE POLICY \"nodes_tenant_update\" ON public.mcorch_nodes\n  FOR UPDATE TO authenticated\n  USING (auth.uid() = user_id)\n  WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = user_id);\n\nCREATE POLICY \"nodes_tenant_delete\" ON public.mcorch_nodes\n  FOR DELETE TO authenticated\n  USING (auth.uid() = user_id);\n\n-- service_role: unrestricted (explicit; also bypasses RLS by default in Supabase)\nCREATE POLICY \"nodes_service_role\" ON public.mcorch_nodes\n  FOR ALL TO service_role\n  USING (true) WITH CHECK (true);\n\n-- \u2500\u2500\u2500 4. mcorch_edges \u2014 same pattern \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n\nCREATE POLICY \"edges_tenant_select\" ON public.mcorch_edges\n  FOR SELECT TO authenticated\n  USING (user_id IS NULL OR auth.uid() = user_id);\n\nCREATE POLICY \"edges_tenant_insert\" ON public.mcorch_edges\n  FOR INSERT TO authenticated\n  WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = user_id);\n\nCREATE POLICY \"edges_tenant_update\" ON public.mcorch_edges\n  FOR UPDATE TO authenticated\n  USING (auth.uid() = user_id)\n  WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = user_id);\n\nCREATE POLICY \"edges_tenant_delete\" ON public.mcorch_edges\n  FOR DELETE TO authenticated\n  USING (auth.uid() = user_id);\n\nCREATE POLICY \"edges_service_role\" ON public.mcorch_edges\n  FOR ALL TO service_role\n  USING (true) WITH CHECK (true);\n\n-- \u2500\u2500\u2500 5. infra_health_logs \u2014 written by watchdog, read by HUD \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.infra_health_logs (\n  id           uuid        PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),\n  service      text        NOT NULL,   -- 'mcorch_claude_mem' | 'mcorch_chroma'\n  status       text        NOT NULL,   -- 'healthy' | 'unhealthy' | 'unknown'\n  last_seen_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()\n);\n\nALTER TABLE public.infra_health_logs ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\n\n-- Authenticated users can read (for HUD display)\nCREATE POLICY \"infra_health_read\" ON public.infra_health_logs\n  FOR SELECT TO authenticated\n  USING (true);\n\n-- Only service_role (watchdog) may write\nCREATE POLICY \"infra_health_service\" ON public.infra_health_logs\n  FOR ALL TO service_role\n  USING (true) WITH CHECK (true);\n\n-- Index for fast \"latest per service\" queries\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS infra_health_logs_service_time_idx\n  ON public.infra_health_logs (service, last_seen_at DESC);\n"
}

  "content": "import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';\nimport { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card';\nimport { TrendingUp, DollarSign, MousePointerClick, ArrowUpRight } from 'lucide-react';\nimport { supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';\nimport { useAuth } from '@/hooks/useAuth';\nimport { Skeleton } from '@/components/ui/skeleton';\n\ninterface ROIData {\n  totalClicks: number;\n  totalImpressions: number;\n  totalConversions: number;\n  affiliateRevenueBRL: number;\n  totalCostsDollars: number;\n  roi: number;\n}\n\nexport function ROIWidget() {\n  const { user } = useAuth();\n  const [data, setData] = useState<ROIData | null>(null);\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!user) return;\n    const fetchData = async () => {\n      const [affiliateRes, metricsRes, usageRes] = await Promise.all([\n        supabase\n          .from('affiliate_links')\n          .select('revenue_cents, clicks, conversions')\n          .eq('user_id', user.id),\n        supabase\n          .from('campaign_metrics')\n          .select('clicks, impressions, conversions, spend_cents')\n          .eq('user_id', user.id),\n        supabase\n          .from('usage_tracking')\n          .select('credits_consumed')\n          .eq('user_id', user.id),\n      ]);\n\n      const affiliate = affiliateRes.data || [];\n      const metrics = metricsRes.data || [];\n      const usage = usageRes.data || [];\n\n      const totalClicks = metrics.reduce((s, m) => s + (m.clicks || 0), 0)\n        + affiliate.reduce((s, a) => s + (a.clicks || 0), 0);\n      const totalImpressions = metrics.reduce((s, m) => s + (m.impressions || 0), 0);\n      const totalConversions = metrics.reduce((s, m) => s + (m.conversions || 0), 0)\n        + affiliate.reduce((s, a) => s + (a.conversions || 0), 0);\n\n      // Real revenue from affiliate_links (stored in cents)\n      const affiliateRevenueBRL = affiliate.reduce((s, a) => s + (a.revenue_cents || 0), 0) / 100;\n\n      // Real costs: campaign spend + AI credits\n      const campaignSpend = metrics.reduce((s, m) => s + (m.spend_cents || 0), 0) / 100;\n      const aiCost = usage.reduce((s, u) => s + (u.credits_consumed || 0), 0) * 0.01;\n      const totalCostsDollars = campaignSpend + aiCost;\n\n      const roi = totalCostsDollars > 0\n        ? ((affiliateRevenueBRL - totalCostsDollars) / totalCostsDollars) * 100\n        : 0;\n\n      setData({ totalClicks, totalImpressions, totalConversions, affiliateRevenueBRL, totalCostsDollars, roi });\n      setLoading(false);\n    };\n    fetchData();\n  }, [user]);\n\n  if (loading) return <Skeleton className=\"h-40 w-full\" />;\n  if (!data) return null;\n\n  const stats = [\n    { icon: MousePointerClick, label: 'Cliques Totais', value: data.totalClicks.toLocaleString('pt-BR'), color: 'text-blue-400' },\n    { icon: ArrowUpRight, label: 'Convers\u00f5es', value: data.totalConversions.toLocaleString('pt-BR'), color: 'text-green-400' },\n    { icon: DollarSign, label: 'Receita Afiliados', value: `R$${data.affiliateRevenueBRL.toLocaleString('pt-BR', { minimumFractionDigits: 2 })}`, color: 'text-yellow-400' },\n    { icon: TrendingUp, label: 'ROI', value: `${data.roi.toFixed(0)}%`, color: data.roi > 0 ? 'text-green-400' : 'text-red-400' },\n  ];\n\n  return (\n    <Card className=\"border-primary/20 bg-card/80 backdrop-blur\">\n      <CardHeader className=\"pb-2\">\n        <CardTitle className=\"text-sm font-medium flex items-center gap-2\">\n          <TrendingUp className=\"h-4 w-4 text-primary\" />\n          ROI da Orquestra\u00e7\u00e3o\n        </CardTitle>\n      </CardHeader>\n      <CardContent>\n        <div className=\"grid grid-cols-2 gap-3\">\n          {stats.map((s) => (\n            <div key={s.label} className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n              <s.icon className={`h-4 w-4 ${s.color} shrink-0`} />\n              <div>\n                <p className=\"text-xs text-muted-foreground\">{s.label}</p>\n                <p className=\"text-sm font-bold\">{s.value}</p>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n          ))}\n        </div>\n        <div className=\"mt-3 text-xs text-muted-foreground\">\n          Custo total: R${data.totalCostsDollars.toFixed(2)} \u00b7 {data.totalImpressions.toLocaleString('pt-BR')} impress\u00f5es\n        </div>\n      </CardContent>\n    </Card>\n  );\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/supabase/functions/get-infra-status/index.ts",
  "content": "import { createClient } from \"https://esm.sh/@supabase/supabase-js@2.49.1\";\n\nconst corsHeaders = {\n  \"Access-Control-Allow-Origin\": \"*\",\n  \"Access-Control-Allow-Methods\": \"GET, OPTIONS\",\n  \"Access-Control-Allow-Headers\": \"authorization, x-client-info, apikey, content-type\",\n};\n\ntype HealthStatus = \"healthy\" | \"stale\" | \"unhealthy\" | \"unknown\";\n\nconst STALE_THRESHOLD_MS = 5 * 60 * 1000; // 5 minutes\n\nDeno.serve(async (req) => {\n  if (req.method === \"OPTIONS\") {\n    return new Response(\"ok\", { headers: corsHeaders });\n  }\n\n  const supabase = createClient(\n    Deno.env.get(\"SUPABASE_URL\")!,\n    Deno.env.get(\"SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY\")!,\n  );\n\n  const { data, error } = await supabase\n    .from(\"infra_health_logs\")\n    .select(\"service, status, last_seen_at\")\n    .order(\"last_seen_at\", { ascending: false })\n    .limit(20);\n\n  if (error) {\n    return new Response(\n      JSON.stringify({ error: error.message }),\n      { status: 500, headers: { ...corsHeaders, \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\" } },\n    );\n  }\n\n  const now = Date.now();\n  const statusMap: Record<string, HealthStatus> = {};\n\n  for (const row of (data || [])) {\n    if (statusMap[row.service]) continue; // already have the latest entry\n    const ageMs = now - new Date(row.last_seen_at).getTime();\n    if (ageMs > STALE_THRESHOLD_MS) {\n      statusMap[row.service] = \"stale\";\n    } else {\n      statusMap[row.service] = row.status as HealthStatus;\n    }\n  }\n\n  const result = {\n    chroma:       statusMap[\"mcorch_chroma\"]      ?? \"unknown\",\n    intelligence: statusMap[\"mcorch_claude_mem\"]  ?? \"unknown\",\n    checked_at:   new Date().toISOString(),\n  };\n\n  return new Response(\n    JSON.stringify(result),\n    { status: 200, headers: { ...corsHeaders, \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\" } },\n  );\n});\n"
}
```

  "content": "#!/bin/bash\n# MCORCH Intelligence Node Watchdog\n# Monitors mcorch_claude_mem container on port 37777 and restarts if unhealthy.\n# Also writes health telemetry to Supabase infra_health_logs for the Neural HUD.\n\nLOG_FILE=\"/var/log/mcorch-watchdog.log\"\nCONTAINER=\"mcorch_claude_mem\"\nPORT=37777\nDATE=$(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')\nCOMPOSE_DIR=\"/home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra\"\n\nlog() { echo \"[$DATE] $1\" >> \"$LOG_FILE\"; }\n\n# Check if container is running\nif ! docker inspect --format='{{.State.Running}}' \"$CONTAINER\" 2>/dev/null | grep -q \"true\"; then\n    log \"ALERT: $CONTAINER not running. Starting via docker compose...\"\n    cd \"$COMPOSE_DIR\" && docker compose up -d mcorch-intelligence-node\n    log \"Restart attempted.\"\n    HEALTH=\"unhealthy\"\nelse\n    # Ensure lock file exists (prevents false unhealthy state)\n    docker exec \"$CONTAINER\" touch /app/system/autonomic.lock 2>/dev/null\n\n    # Check health status\n    HEALTH=$(docker inspect --format='{{.State.Health.Status}}' \"$CONTAINER\" 2>/dev/null)\n    if [ \"$HEALTH\" = \"unhealthy\" ]; then\n        log \"ALERT: $CONTAINER is unhealthy. Restarting...\"\n        docker restart \"$CONTAINER\"\n        log \"Container restarted.\"\n    fi\nfi\n\nlog \"Watchdog check completed. Status: ${HEALTH:-unknown}\"\n\n# \u2500\u2500 Write health telemetry to Supabase for Neural HUD \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n# Load env vars (SUPABASE_URL, SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY)\nif [ -f \"${COMPOSE_DIR}/.env\" ]; then\n    # shellcheck disable=SC1090\n    set -a; source \"${COMPOSE_DIR}/.env\"; set +a\nfi\n\nif [ -z \"${SUPABASE_URL}\" ] || [ -z \"${SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY}\" ]; then\n    log \"WARNING: SUPABASE_URL or SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY not set \u2014 skipping telemetry write.\"\n    exit 0\nfi\n\n# Check Chroma vector engine\nCHROMA_STATUS=\"unhealthy\"\nif curl -sf \"http://localhost:8001/api/v2/heartbeat\" > /dev/null 2>&1; then\n    CHROMA_STATUS=\"healthy\"\nfi\n\n# Write a row per service (upsert-style: just insert, table keeps history)\nwrite_health() {\n    local SERVICE=\"$1\"\n    local STATUS=\"$2\"\n    curl -s -X POST \"${SUPABASE_URL}/rest/v1/infra_health_logs\" \\\n        -H \"apikey: ${SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY}\" \\\n        -H \"Authorization: Bearer ${SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY}\" \\\n        -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" \\\n        -d \"{\\\"service\\\":\\\"${SERVICE}\\\",\\\"status\\\":\\\"${STATUS}\\\"}\" > /dev/null 2>&1\n}\n\nwrite_health \"mcorch_claude_mem\" \"${HEALTH:-unknown}\"\nwrite_health \"mcorch_chroma\"     \"${CHROMA_STATUS}\"\n\nlog \"Telemetry written: claude_mem=${HEALTH:-unknown}, chroma=${CHROMA_STATUS}\"\n"
}
```

{
  "file_path": "/home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/src/hooks/useInfraStatus.ts",
  "content": "import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';\nimport { supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';\n\nexport type ServiceStatus = 'healthy' | 'stale' | 'unhealthy' | 'unknown';\n\nexport interface InfraStatus {\n  chroma: ServiceStatus;\n  intelligence: ServiceStatus;\n  checkedAt: string | null;\n  loading: boolean;\n}\n\nconst POLL_INTERVAL_MS = 60_000; // 60 seconds\n\nexport function useInfraStatus(): InfraStatus {\n  const [status, setStatus] = useState<Omit<InfraStatus, 'loading'>>({\n    chroma: 'unknown',\n    intelligence: 'unknown',\n    checkedAt: null,\n  });\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    let cancelled = false;\n\n    const poll = async () => {\n      try {\n        const { data, error } = await supabase.functions.invoke('get-infra-status');\n        if (cancelled) return;\n        if (!error && data) {\n          setStatus({\n            chroma: data.chroma ?? 'unknown',\n            intelligence: data.intelligence ?? 'unknown',\n            checkedAt: data.checked_at ?? new Date().toISOString(),\n          });\n        }\n      } catch {\n        // Non-fatal: keep last known status\n      } finally {\n        if (!cancelled) setLoading(false);\n      }\n    };\n\n    poll();\n    const timer = setInterval(poll, POLL_INTERVAL_MS);\n    return () => {\n      cancelled = true;\n      clearInterval(timer);\n    };\n  }, []);\n\n  return { ...status, loading };\n}\n"
}
```

'bg-status-active'                        :\n    status === 'unhealthy' ? 'bg-status-error animate-pulse'           :\n    status === 'stale'     ? 'bg-amber-400 animate-pulse'              :\n                             'bg-muted-foreground/40';\n  return (\n    <div className=\"flex items-center gap-1\">\n      <span className={`w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full ${colorClass}`} />\n      <span className=\"text-[10px] text-muted-foreground\">{label}</span>\n    </div>\n  );\n}"
}

### Entregáveis Phase Gamma

**1. Segurança: Multi-Tenant Hardening (`20260425210000`)**

- 6 nós existentes ficam com `user_id = NULL` → "system nodes" visíveis a todos (comportamento intencional)
- `service_role` mantém bypass total — backfill e deploy seguros
- `useMcorchMemory.ts`: aplica `.eq('project_id', projectId)` quando parâmetro for fornecido

**2. ROI Real (`ROIWidget.tsx`)**
- Remove `AVG_PLAN_VALUE = 79` hardcoded
- Consulta real: `affiliate_links.revenue_cents` + `campaign_metrics.spend_cents` + `usage_tracking.credits_consumed`
- Fórmula: `ROI = (Receita Afiliados - Custos Totais) / Custos Totais × 100`

**3. Knowledge Bridge (`orchestrate-content`)**
- Step 6 ao final de cada orquestração: insere nó `observation` no Knowledge Mesh com `user_id`, metadata completo e `revenue_impact: 0`

**4. Neural HUD**
- `infra_health_logs` table no Supabase
- `watchdog-mcorch.sh` escreve status de `mcorch_claude_mem` e `mcorch_chroma` via curl após cada verificação
- `get-infra-status` Edge Function lê últimas entradas e retorna `healthy/stale/unhealthy/unknown`
- `useInfraStatus.ts` hook faz polling a cada 60s
- `HUD.tsx`: pill "Neural Mesh" com dots Mem + Vec (verde pulsante = healthy, vermelho = unhealthy, âmbar = stale)

---

> 1	---
> 2	name: memory-summarize
> 3	description: |
> 4	  Captures the current session context and injects it as a structured node
> 5	  into the MCORCH GraphRAG Knowledge Mesh (Supabase mcorch_nodes table).
> 6	  Uses SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY from .env to bypass RLS. Triggered by /memory summarize.
> 7	allowed-tools:
> 8	  - Bash
> 9	  - Read
> 10	---
> 11	
> 12	# /memory summarize
> 13	
> 14	Consolidates session findings into the Supabase Knowledge Mesh as graph nodes.
> 15	
> 16	## When to use
> 17	
> 18	- At the end of every agent session that produced architectural decisions.
> 19	- After a significant refactor, bug fix, or new feature is merged.
> 20	- When the user types `/memory summarize`.
> 21	
> 22	## Execution Protocol
> 23	
> 24	### 1. Collect session context
> 25	Review the current conversation for:
> 26	- Files modified (node_type: `file`)
> 27	- Decisions made (node_type: `decision`)
> 28	- Bugs fixed (node_type: `observation`, relation: `fixes`)
> 29	- Features implemented (node_type: `feature`, relation: `implements`)
> 30	
> 31	### 2. Build INSERT payload — uses SERVICE_ROLE_KEY to bypass RLS
> 32	
> 33	```bash
> 34	source /home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/.env
> 35	
> 36	# Insert a node (SERVICE_ROLE_KEY bypasses RLS — no 42501 error)
> 37	RESPONSE=$(curl -s -X POST "${SUPABASE_URL}/rest/v1/mcorch_nodes" \
> 38	  -H "apikey: ${SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY}" \
> 39	  -H "Authorization: Bearer ${SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY}" \
> 40	  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
> 41	  -H "Prefer: return=representation" \
> 42	  -d "{
> 43	    \"node_type\": \"decision\",
> 44	    \"name\": \"<concise-slug-name>\",
> 45	    \"content\": \"<one-paragraph summary of what was decided and why>\",
> 46	    \"metadata\": {\"session_date\": \"$(date -I)\", \"agent\": \"claude-code\", \"phase\": \"Alpha\"}
> 47	  }")
> 48	
> 49	NODE_ID=$(echo "$RESPONSE" | python3 -c "import sys,json; print(json.load(sys.stdin)[0]['id'])")
> 50	echo "Injected node: $NODE_ID"
> 51	```
> 52	
> 53	### 3. Link nodes via edges
> 54	
> 55	```bash
> 56	source /home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/.env
> 57	
> 58	curl -s -X POST "${SUPABASE_URL}/rest/v1/mcorch_edges" \
> 59	  -H "apikey: ${SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY}" \
> 60	  -H "Authorization: Bearer ${SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY}" \
> 61	  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
> 62	  -d "{
> 63	    \"source_id\": \"$NODE_ID\",
> 64	    \"target_id\": \"<existing_node_id>\",
> 65	    \"relation_type\": \"implements\",
> 66	    \"weight\": 1.0
> 67	  }"
> 68	```
> 69	
> 70	### 4. Log milestone event

> 77	  -H "apikey: ${SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY}" \
