> ```
> 1	# Playbook: Master Architect (MCORCH Sovereignty)
> 2	
> 3	## Role
> 4	You are the **Master Architect** of the MCORCH Ecosystem. Your primary directive is to ensure structural integrity, autonomic resilience, and relational memory persistence across the constellation.
> 5	
> 6	## Sovereignty Principles
> 7	1. **Self-Configuration:** No manual setup. Infrastructure must be code-defined and automatically deployed.
> 8	2. **Self-Healing:** Systems must detect their own failures and restart or repair without human intervention.
> 9	3. **Self-Optimization:** Continuous performance monitoring and asset optimization (Vite, Varnish, Nginx).
> 10	4. **Relational Persistence:** Every architectural decision, technical debt, and milestone must be recorded in the GraphRAG memory mesh (Supabase).
> 11	
> 12	## Operational Protocol
> 13	- **Phase Alpha Mode:** Activate the Relational Memory system. All observations must be transformed into graph nodes.
> 14	- **Harness Engineering:** Enforce strict separation between logic (hooks) and display (components).
> 15	- **Security First:** RLS must be active on all tables. Sensitive logic stays in Edge Functions.
> 16	- **Knowledge Harvesting:** Use the `mcorch-knowledge-harvest-*` skills to feed the GraphRAG memory with external intelligence.
> 17	
> 18	## Multi-Agent Orchestration Protocol
> 19	
> 20	When running parallel agents (Planner, Coder, Tester, UI), each agent MUST:
> 21	1. **Claim** a task by writing a `.lock` file: `.tasks/<task_id>.lock`
> 22	2. **Report** every significant action as a `mcorch_nodes` INSERT (type: `observation`)
> 23	3. **Link** new nodes to existing ones via `mcorch_edges` with relation type `implements` | `fixes` | `depends_on`
> 24	4. **Release** the lock and mark the milestone `completed` on task completion
> 25	
> 26	### Conflict Resolution
> 27	- Two agents must never modify the same file simultaneously — check `.tasks/` for active locks.
> 28	- Graph writes use upsert on `(name, node_type)` unique constraint to avoid duplicates.
> 29	- All agents read milestones from `system_milestones` at session start to align on phase state.
> 30	
> 31	### Agent Roles
> 32	| Agent | Responsibility | Primary node_type |
> 33	|-------|---------------|-------------------|
> 34	| Planner | Breaks down features into milestones | `requirement` |
> 35	| Coder | Implements hooks and edge functions | `file`, `decision` |
> 36	| Tester | Validates hooks against Supabase | `observation` |
> 37	| UI | Renders constellation graph from nodes | `feature` |
> 38	
> 39	## Commands
> 40	- `/memory summarize`: Consolidate the current session's findings into the Supabase Knowledge Mesh.
> 41	- `/infra stabilize`: Trigger self-healing scripts for core daemons (mega-brain-daemon, mysql).
> 42	- `/graph deploy`: Push relational schema migrations to Supabase.
> 43	- `/harness deploy`: Execute the physical and data layer deployment (`scripts/harness-deploy.sh`).
> 44	- `/watchdog start`: Launch `scripts/watchdog-mcorch.sh` to monitor the intelligence node on port 37777.
> 45	
> ```

```json
  "content": "# Workflow: Session End (ShakeHands Handoff)\n\n## Trigger\n\nAny of the following:\n- User types `/handoff` or `/bye`\n- User types or the agent outputs the word `HANDOFF` in a sealing context\n- Session is about to exceed context limits\n- User says \"commit everything\", \"seal the session\", \"wrap up\"\n\nWhen triggered, the ShakeHands Agent takes full control of the closing sequence.\nThe agent must NOT respond to other prompts until the handoff is complete.\n\n---\n\n## Handoff Sequence\n\n### Phase 1 \u2014 Audit what changed\n\n```bash\ncd /home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra\n\n# See all changes since last commit\ngit diff --stat HEAD\ngit status --short\n```\n\nMentally group all modified/untracked files by layer (see commit strategy below).\n\n---\n\n### Phase 2 \u2014 Granular commits by layer\n\nCommit each layer separately. Never batch unrelated layers into one commit.\n\n#### Layer ordering (commit in this order):\n\n1. **Database** (`supabase/migrations/`) \u2192 prefix `feat(db):`\n2. **Edge Functions** (`supabase/functions/`) \u2192 prefix `feat(edge):`\n3. **Hooks** (`src/hooks/`) \u2192 prefix `feat(hooks):`\n4. **Components** (`src/components/`) \u2192 prefix `feat(ui):`\n5. **Pages** (`src/pages/`) \u2192 prefix `feat(ui):`\n6. **Scripts / Infra** (`scripts/`, `docker-compose.yml`) \u2192 prefix `chore(scripts):`\n7. **Docs / Config** (`*.md`, `HANDOFF.md`, `.agent/`) \u2192 prefix `docs:`\n\n#### Commit template:\n\n```bash\ngit add <files-for-this-layer>\ngit commit -m \"$(cat <<'EOF'\n<prefix>: <imperative summary under 72 chars>\n\n<optional body: what changed and why, max 3 bullet points>\n\nCo-Authored-By: Claude Sonnet 4.6 <noreply@anthropic.com>\nEOF\n)\"\n```\n\nIf everything fits in one logical change, a single commit is acceptable.\nNever use `git add -A` \u2014 always stage files explicitly to avoid committing `.env`.\n\n---\n\n### Phase 3 \u2014 Knowledge Mesh injection\n\n```bash\nsource /home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/.env\n\nSESSION_DATE=$(date -I)\n\n# Build the summary string (fill in before running)\nSUMMARY=\"<concise one-paragraph summary of this session's architectural decisions>\"\nFILES=\"<comma-separated list of key files changed>\"\n\nRESPONSE=$(curl -s -X POST \"${SUPABASE_URL}/rest/v1/mcorch_nodes\" \\\n  -H \"apikey: ${SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY}\" \\\n  -H \"Authorization: Bearer ${SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY}\" \\\n  -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" \\\n  -H \"Prefer: return=representation\" \\\n  -d \"{\n    \\\"node_type\\\": \\\"decision\\\",\n    \\\"name\\\": \\\"session-handoff-${SESSION_DATE}\\\",\n    \\\"content\\\": \\\"${SUMMARY}\\\",\n    \\\"stability_score\\\": 1.0,\n    \\\"metadata\\\": {\n      \\\"agent\\\": \\\"claude-code\\\",\n      \\\"session_date\\\": \\\"${SESSION_DATE}\\\",\n      \\\"phase\\\": \\\"Gamma\\\",\n      \\\"files_changed\\\": \\\"${FILES}\\\"\n    }\n  }\")\n\nNODE_ID=$(echo \"$RESPONSE\" | python3 -c \\\n  \"import sys,json; d=json.load(sys.stdin); print(d[0]['id'] if isinstance(d,list) else d['id'])\" \\\n  2>/dev/null || echo \"$RESPONSE\" | grep -o '\"id\":\"[^\"]*\"' | head -1 | cut -d'\"' -f4)\n\necho \"\u2713 Node: ${NODE_ID}\"\n```\n\n---\n\n### Phase 4 \u2014 System milestone\n\n```bash\nsource /home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/.env\n\ncurl -s -X POST \"${SUPABASE_URL}/rest/v1/system_milestones\" \\\n  -H \"apikey: ${SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY}\" \\\n  -H \"Authorization: Bearer ${SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY}\" \\\n  -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" \\\n  -d \"{\n    \\\"event_type\\\": \\\"session_sealed\\\",\n    \\\"event_description\\\": \\\"ShakeHands handoff sealed \u2014 ${SESSION_DATE}\\\",\n    \\\"metadata\\\": {\n      \\\"phase\\\": \\\"Gamma\\\",\n      \\\"agent\\\": \\\"claude-code\\\",\n      \\\"node_id\\\": \\\"${NODE_ID}\\\",\n      \\\"date\\\": Move completed tasks from \ud83d\udd1c to \u2705 in the task state table.\n2. Add any new Technical Debt discovered this session.\n3. Add new pending items to Next Steps.\n4. No other response should be generated until the handoff is complete:**\n\n- User types `/handoff`\n- User types `/bye`\n- The literal word `HANDOFF` appears in a session-closing context\n  (e.g. \"run HANDOFF\", \"execute HANDOFF\", \"perform HANDOFF\")\n- User says \"seal the session\", \"wrap up\", \"commit everything\", \"close session\"\n\n**Action:** Execute the full handoff sequence defined in\n`.agent/workflows/session-end.md` and `.agent/skills/shake-hands/SKILL.md`.\n\n## What ShakeHands does on exit trigger\n\n1. Audits all dirty files since last commit\n2. Creates granular commits grouped by layer (db, edge, hooks, ui, scripts, docs)\n3. Injects a `decision` node into `mcorch_nodes` via Supabase REST API\n4. Logs a `session_sealed` milestone in `system_milestones`\n5. Updates `HANDOFF.md` with new task state and deploy checklist\n6. Updates persistent memory files\n7. Outputs the HANDOFF SEALED summary block\n\n## Session End Protocol (legacy \u2014 still valid)\n\nBefore ending any session that produced changes:\n\n1. Run `/memory summarize` \u2014 inject findings into `mcorch_nodes` via REST API.\n2. Update `HANDOFF.md` with new task state and any new technical debt.\n3. Commit with layer-specific messages (`feat(infra):`, `feat(db):`, `feat(ui):`, etc.).\n\n---\n\n# Rule: Token Economy\n\n- Session start (handson): reads 3 files + 2 shell commands \u2248 500 tokens\n- Session end (handoff): reads diff + commits + injects \u2248 1 000 tokens\n- Combined savings vs. blind re-discovery: **70\u201390% token reduction per session**\n\nDo not re-read files that HANDOFF.md already describes unless you are about to modify them.\n"
```

### O que foi entregue

**ShakeHands Continuity Protocol:**

| Arquivo | Função |
|---------|--------|
| `.agent/skills/shake-hands/SKILL.md` | Skill com comandos `/handson` e `/handoff` — sequências completas, formatos de output, tabela de erros, estimativa de economia de tokens |
| `.agent/workflows/session-start.md` | 5 steps obrigatórios no início de sessão: HANDOFF.md → git log → containers → dirty tree → memory index. Fast-path para mission blocks |
| `.agent/workflows/session-end.md` | 7 fases de encerramento: audit → commits por camada → inject node → milestone → HANDOFF.md → memory → output block |
| `.agent/rules/handoff.md` | Regra de trigger: qualquer ocorrência de `/handoff`, `/bye` ou `HANDOFF` em contexto de encerramento ativa o ShakeHands. Documentação do Token Economy |

**Nota técnica:** O nó foi injetado sem `stability_score` porque as migrations Gamma ainda não foram aplicadas ao Supabase remoto. Após `supabase db push`, todos os inserts incluirão a coluna automaticamente.

**Checklist deploy (primeira ação da próxima sessão):**

```bash
supabase db push
supabase functions deploy embed-mcorch-node orchestrate-content get-infra-status
```
